## MONITORED ASSUMPTIONS (7 items)

### MONITOR-001:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-13
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-003
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:**
"Independent FOR/AGAINST search prevents confirmation bias"

**Why monitoring:**
While the independent search mechanism is theoretically defensible, strong evidence suggests role assignment (FOR/AGAINST) creates motivated reasoning and amplifies bias rather than preventing it. Role activation is a known psychological phenomenon that may undermine the intended debiasing mechanism.

**What would change disposition:**
- Evidence that role-assignment does NOT activate motivated reasoning in this architecture
- Empirical comparison of bias metrics in 15a vs. 15b outputs showing no role-based bias amplification
- Implementation of debiasing protocols that mitigate role activation effects

**Priority:** HIGH

**Cadence:** Weekly

**Next check:** 2026-05-03

**Monitoring approach:**
- Analyze 15a and 15b outputs for systematic bias patterns
- Compare quality/objectivity metrics between FOR and AGAINST agents
- Look for evidence of motivated reasoning in search selection or interpretation

**Cycle count:** 1
**Last checked:** 2026-04-26 (RE-TRIGGER by 15d — cycle 1)

**Evidence trajectory:**
- Supporting: stable (baseline — no prior data)
- Challenging: stable (baseline — no prior data)
- New sources since last cycle: N/A (first cycle)

**Status:** ACTIVE

**Risk flags:** CRITICAL (Role-based bias amplification is foundational risk in 15a/15b architecture)

**PROVENANCE:**
Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a→15a,15b→15c→15d]; Current status: MONITORING (cycle 0)

### MONITOR-019:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-13
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-015
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:**
"Running a potentially biased pipeline (FOR/AGAINST) is better than not running one at all."

**Why monitoring:**
Genuinely contested — strong evidence both directions. NIH research shows expertise-driven bias weakly dominates ignorance (15a: Strong support). But anchoring/epistemic pollution research shows biased preliminary results contaminate downstream decisions (15b: Strong challenge). Critical nuance: 15a's support concerns individual expert bias; 15b's challenge concerns structural/systematic bias — potentially different phenomena.

**What would change disposition:**
- If independent (non-FOR/AGAINST) evaluation of sample items yields >30% different dispositions → REVISE (bias is decision-relevant)
- If dispositions stable across methods → INCORPORATE
- If debiasing protocols successfully mitigate anchoring → INCORPORATE with caveats

**Priority:** HIGH (epistemic integrity of entire pipeline depends on this)

**Cadence:** Weekly

**Next check:** 2026-05-03

**Monitoring approach:**
- Compare pipeline outputs against neutral single-agent evaluation on same items
- Track whether disposition consumers treat results as hypothesis-generating (safe) or decision-gating (risky)

### MONITOR-020:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-13
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-016
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:**
"Literature search results should gate implementation decisions (Phase 2a pause)."

**Why monitoring:**
Framework commitment with legitimate tension. Evidence-gated development is standard practice in research-informed design (15a: Moderate support). But analysis paralysis is well-documented and agile iteration is strongly supported as alternative (15b: Strong challenge). Context-dependent: gating appropriate for irreversible high-stakes decisions, counterproductive for reversible experimental deployments.

**What would change disposition:**
- If pause extends beyond 2 weeks without actionable decisions → REVISE (analysis paralysis applies)
- If pause leads to design changes that prevent downstream failures → INCORPORATE
- If Phase 2a proceeds and generates empirical data more valuable than literature → REVISE

**Priority:** HIGH (directly affects Phase 2a timeline)

**Cadence:** Weekly

**Next check:** 2026-05-03

**Monitoring approach:**
- Track pause duration and whether actionable decisions result
- Compare cost of delay against risks identified in literature search
- Note whether Phase 2a is reversible (favoring iteration) or irreversible (favoring gating)

**Cycle count:** 1

### MONITOR-053:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-20
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-049
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:** "Session = one agent run" (the caching-architecture's unit of cacheable work is a single agent invocation)

**Why monitoring:**
15a SUPPORTED (Strong) — vendor-documented cache-scope semantics (Anthropic, OpenAI) treat session-level boundaries as the natural unit for prefix reuse; well-aligned with standard caching-architecture practice. 15b PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate) — literature on session-definition (Fielding 2000; REST/HTTP session semantics) and multi-agent orchestration (Wooldridge 2009) notes that "one session = one agent run" under-models conversational carryover and multi-turn composition; a hybrid/spill policy (allow cache carryover within a logical work-unit spanning 1-N runs) would fit the observed workflow better. The presumption is defensible as a first-cut but will need a hybrid-policy specification once workload patterns stabilize.

**What would change disposition:**
- Incorporate: Hybrid-policy specified (e.g., "session = agent run, but cache may persist across runs within the same work-unit under explicit rules"); OR empirical observation that strict 1:1 mapping holds over 4+ weeks with no cross-run cache-carry desire.
- Revise: Observed workflow pattern where strict 1:1 mapping produces material cache-miss cost (e.g., >10% of expected savings forfeited to session boundary); OR a tradition-wide review-task naturally spans N runs and forfeits prefix reuse.

**Priority:** MEDIUM (caching-rollout 2026-04-27 depends on session-definition working; hybrid-policy is a cheap fix if needed)

**Cadence:** Weekly for 4 weeks post-rollout (2026-04-27 onward), then monthly if stable

**Next check:** 2026-05-04 (one week post-rollout)

**Monitoring approach:**
- Instrument cache-miss events per session; classify by cause (session-boundary, prefix-change, other)
- Track whether any workflows naturally want cross-run cache-carry and are prevented by the 1:1 mapping
- Audit for emergent multi-run work-units that would benefit from a hybrid policy
- Coordinate with ASSUMPTION-050's precondition-audit monitoring

**Cycle count:** 0
**Last checked:** 2026-04-20 (initialized)
**Evidence trajectory:**
- Supporting: stable (baseline — vendor-documented)
- Challenging: stable (baseline — hybrid-policy case is theoretical until observed)
- New sources since last cycle: N/A (first cycle)

**Status:** ACTIVE

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b → 15a, 15b → 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

**Cluster note:** CACHING-ARCHITECTURE cluster — session-definition contract pairs with ASSUMPTION-050 (static prefix), ASSUMPTION-053 (appended-turn topology), and ASSUMPTION-054 (byte-stability) as the architectural foundation.

### MONITOR-054:

**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-050
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:** "Static prefix for the caching architecture is the 49 RC Wiki files (at load time)"

**Why monitoring:**
15a SUPPORTED (Strong) — a defined, well-scoped set of static-prefix files is exactly the structure prompt-caching wants; Anthropic/OpenAI docs explicitly recommend this pattern. 15b PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate) — four unaudited preconditions identified: (1) ordering stability of the 49 files (any reorder invalidates cache), (2) encoding/whitespace/line-ending stability (silent drift from editor configs can flip hashes), (3) symlink/path-resolution stability (macOS HFS+ case-insensitivity edge cases), (4) file-content churn rate (pairs with PRESUMPTION-057 REVISE). The 49-file set is correct in principle but the preconditions need a pre-rollout audit.

**What would change disposition:**
- Incorporate: Pre-rollout audit confirms all 4 preconditions hold (deterministic ordering, byte-stable encoding, stable path resolution, churn rate below invalidation threshold); OR byte-stability smoke test (ASSUMPTION-054) extended to cover these preconditions passes.
- Revise: Any of the 4 preconditions fails audit; OR observed cache-miss rate post-rollout exceeds projection due to prefix-content instability.

**Priority:** MEDIUM-HIGH (directly gates the 2026-04-27 caching rollout; cheap audit cost)

**Cadence:** Pre-rollout audit (by 2026-04-26); then weekly for 4 weeks post-rollout; then monthly

**Next check:** 2026-05-03

**Monitoring approach:**
- Run pre-rollout audit on the 4 preconditions (ordering determinism, encoding stability, path-resolution, churn rate)
- Post-rollout: instrument cache-miss events attributable to prefix drift
- Pair with PRESUMPTION-057 REVISE remediation (git-log churn audit)
- Pair with ASSUMPTION-054 MONITOR / REVISE (byte-stability smoke test extension)

The 70-80% midpoint is consistent with a well-behaved workload. 15b PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate) — cost projection depends on three unaudited preconditions: (1) cache-hit rate (gated by PRESUMPTION-057 churn audit), (2) prefix-length-to-suffix-length ratio (actual savings scale with the cacheable-portion share of the token bill), (3) cache-warmup cost amortization (first-call penalty can dominate short-run economics). The number is defensible as an order-of-magnitude expectation but is untested against the specific workload shape.

- Revise: Observed savings outside the 50-90% band post-rollout; OR prefix-length-to-suffix-length ratio measured and found to be materially off the 70-80% projection assumptions; OR cache-warmup penalty dominates for the typical run.

**Priority:** MEDIUM (headline number of the rollout; worth measuring but not blocking)

**Cadence:** Weekly for 4 weeks post-rollout, then monthly

**Next check:** 2026-05-04 (one week post-rollout)

### MONITOR-056:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-20
**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-055
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION

**Statement:** "Binary partition (agents in / agents out) is the correct design shape for the caching architecture"

**Why monitoring:**
15a PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate) — binary partitions are the simplest and most common implementation of prompt-caching boundaries; matches vendor-recommended patterns. 15b PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate) — literature on cache-partitioning (Fowler 2018; CDN cache-segmentation practices) and workload-aware caching (Jain 1991; Dahlin et al. 1994 on cooperative caching) suggests that a ternary or N-ary partition (e.g., always-cached / sometimes-cached / never-cached; or per-agent cache-tier) can yield strictly better cache economics when workload heterogeneity is high. No active deliberation in the architecture deliverables on why binary was chosen over ternary/N-ary; the absence-of-deliberation is the critique, not the choice itself.

**What would change disposition:**
- Incorporate: Brief rationale note added to architecture record ("binary partition chosen because [X]; ternary considered and rejected because [Y]"); OR post-rollout observation shows binary is working and workload is homogeneous enough that N-ary would not help.
- Revise: Observed workload heterogeneity (some agents churn tool definitions, others don't; some agents have much larger prefixes than others) where binary partition forces all-or-nothing cache inclusion and hurts economics.

**Priority:** LOW-MEDIUM (reversible; cheap remediation via rationale note; low-stakes)

**Cadence:** Monthly for 2 months (one post-rollout review cycle + one stability check)

**Next check:** 2026-05-20 (one month post-rollout)

**Monitoring approach:**
- Audit workload heterogeneity across agent types post-rollout (prefix length distribution, tool-definition mutation rate, call frequency)
- Measure cache-economics sensitivity to partition granularity
- Record rationale for partition choice if not yet documented

**Cycle count:** 0
**Last checked:** 2026-04-20 (initialized)
**Evidence trajectory:**
- Supporting: stable (baseline — simplest viable design)
- Challenging: stable (baseline — ternary/N-ary benefit is theoretical until workload heterogeneity measured)

**Status:** ACTIVE

### MONITOR-058:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-21
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-055
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**Statement:** "Phase 6 git commit fails because the sandbox mount topology does not include the repo path at all"

**Why monitoring:**
15a SUPPORTED (Strong) — Docker/OCI mount semantics uniformly confirm that a path not explicitly mounted is not reachable from inside the sandbox; this is the canonical diagnosis for the observed failure mode. 15b PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate) — while the mechanism is correct, the architectural framing smuggles two unverified claims: (a) that the failure is diagnostic of the mount topology rather than a missing bind-mount in a specific scheduled-task config, and (b) that this applies uniformly across scheduled-task runs (see paired PRESUMPTION-061 REVISE). SYSTEMIC-RISK-FLAG raised on the framing-conflation pattern. The diagnosis is correct; the claim that it is stable over time is a separate presumption (PRESUMPTION-061) that was REVISE'd.

**What would change disposition:**
- Incorporate: Pre-flight mount-topology probe instrumented (per PRESUMPTION-061 remediation) and records stable mount topology across >= 10 consecutive runs; AND the specific claim is decomposed — "today's topology does not include repo path" (verifiable) vs. "topology is stable" (separately monitored).
- Revise: Pre-flight probe reveals topology instability (topology differs between runs) OR a second independent cause surfaces for the Phase 6 failure that contradicts the mount-topology-only diagnosis.

**Priority:** MEDIUM-HIGH (directly gates the 2026-04-27 caching rollout Phase 6 commit path; paired with PRESUMPTION-061 REVISE)

**Cadence:** Weekly through 2026-04-27 rollout gate, then monthly

**Next check:** 2026-04-28 (first post-rollout check)

- Track whether mount topology varies across runs (PRESUMPTION-061 pairing)

### MONITOR-062:
**Date queued:** 2026-04-21
**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-068
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION

**Statement:** "Today's successful Chrome MCP double-success represents a resolved auth state rather than a transient window"

**Why monitoring:**
15a NO-SUPPORT-FOUND — no literature supports declaring an opaque auth system "resolved" after 2 successes without root-cause identification. 15b CHALLENGED (Moderate) — symmetric threshold logic argues ASSUMPTION-042's 5-failure threshold for "not transient" implies a 5-success threshold for "resolved"; unknown-cause-outage literature treats no-recorded-remediation as a transience indicator; auth-state opacity prevents direct verification; PRESUMPTION-059 (Chrome singleton) makes false-resolved costly. The challenge is Moderate rather than Strong because today's classification is low-consequence if tomorrow verifies the trend — it becomes high-consequence only if an immediate re-failure catches the system with no escalation primed.

**What would change disposition:**
- Incorporate: 5 consecutive successes observed (ASSUMPTION-042 symmetric threshold); OR observable auth-state checks instrumented (token expiry, session cookies) that independently confirm resolution; OR OPEN-032 pair-remediation implements generalized transience-threshold framework.
- Revise: Chrome MCP fails within the next 5-day window (false-resolved confirmed) with no escalation primed.

**Priority:** MEDIUM (reversible; cheap remediation; ties to Chrome-singleton risk PRESUMPTION-059)

**Cadence:** Daily through 2026-04-26 (until 5-success threshold reached or failure confirms transience)

**Next check:** 2026-04-27

**Monitoring approach:**
- Track consecutive success/failure count for Chrome MCP morning scrape
- Apply ASSUMPTION-042 symmetric threshold logic (5-of-5 for "resolved")
- Coordinate with OPEN-032 transience-threshold generalization
- Instrument any available observable auth-state signals

**Cycle count:** 1 (today is success 2/5)
**Last checked:** 2026-04-26 (RE-TRIGGER by 15d — cycle 1)
**Evidence trajectory:**
- Supporting: stable (2-of-2 successes today)
- Challenging: stable (baseline — auth-state opacity)
- New sources since last cycle: N/A (first cycle)

**Status:** ACTIVE

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b → 15a, 15b → 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

**Cluster note:** OPERATIONAL-DRIFT cluster; ASSUMPTION-042 threshold-symmetry extension; OPEN-032 (generalize transience threshold) is the natural cluster-level fix.

The principled method is supported; the load-bearing concern is cross-platform generalization (speaker-label feature is corpus-specific; YouTube triad will not transfer cleanly to Spotify / RSS-hosted / podcaster-site transcripts). Heuristic exception based on (a) literature support for the method class, not just absence-of-disconfirmation; (b) low blast-radius (per-watch failures are recoverable); (c) fix is cheap (per-platform diagnostic suites). **What would change disposition:**

- REVISE: Pathway 22 trial shows non-Carpathi instance cannot function without Tom-equivalent role and no replacement is specified.

## Batch 2026-05-24 (Agent 15c -- c2a2-lit-search-pipeline scheduled run)

### MONITOR-229:
**Date added:** 2026-05-24
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-220
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-05-30 (joins next scheduled weekly 15d run)

**Statement:** PRS candidates can be validly generated from a talk's announced topic list / runtime metadata when the transcript is unavailable, if confidence is capped at Medium and flagged for transcript verification.

**15a result:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Staged surrogate->candidate extraction with a confidence cap + verification flag is a legitimate PRISMA-style two-pass pattern, and metadata carries real content signal. But validity is contingent on the verification step actually running and on scoping inferences to topic-level fields -- PRS resolution/significance are exactly where proxy error and hallucination concentrate (Pitkin 1999; Boutron 2010; Maynez 2020). Genuinely mixed -> MONITOR.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE once proxy fidelity is measured acceptable on a labeled sample (metadata-derived vs transcript-derived PRS) AND the verification flag is an enforced precondition for incorporation; REVISE if topic-list PRS (esp. resolution/significance) are incorporated without transcript verification.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

---
### MONITOR-230:
**Date added:** 2026-05-24
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-221
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** High
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-05-30 (joins next scheduled weekly 15d run)

**Statement:** C2A2 should locate accountability for its own autonomous ("ownerless") agents in the deployment-and-verification pipeline (Tom's review gate), not in agent-internal predictability.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong, in principle)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate, conditional)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** The design *locus* -- accountability in the oversight/deployment chain rather than in internal predictability -- is strongly supported (Matthias 2004 responsibility gap; Santoni de Sio & van den Hoven 2018 meaningful human control; reinforced by Wolfram computational irreducibility). It is NOT INCORPORATED only because its operative precondition -- an exercised, reason-responsive gate -- is currently unmet (4-day signout; REVISE-050/051; SYSTEMIC-RISK-FLAG I). Per 15c's "err toward MONITOR; premature INCORPORATE is hard to reverse," parked at High pending gate restoration. INCORPORATE-pending-precondition.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE (likely High confidence) once REVISE-050/051 are resolved (gate exercised + SLA/escalation + tracing); REVISE if the design is found to actually depend on agent-internal predictability after all.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING (INCORPORATE-pending-precondition)

### MONITOR-231:
**Date added:** 2026-05-24
**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-241
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION
**Priority:** Low-Medium
**Cadence:** Monthly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-23 (joins next scheduled monthly 15d run)

**Statement:** Firing the full daily cadence on a day with zero human design input presumes daily granularity stays meaningful when there was nothing for a human to have decided.

**15a result:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Real tension, and notably self-illustrating: a daily heartbeat is what surfaced the gate-absence (PRESUMPTION-240/243) that an input-triggered pipeline would have been blind to -- so quiet-day firing has genuine absence/drift-detection value. But a *full* pipeline run over-samples a signal that did not change (Nyquist/observation-design reasoning) and risks self-referential drift/metric theatre (the system processing its own automated output). Mixed -> MONITOR (Low-Med, Monthly).

**What would change disposition:** RESOLVE toward a "lightweight quiet-day heartbeat vs full input-day run" split if quiet-day information yield is ~0 except absence/drift signals; REVISE only if daily runs are shown to manufacture spurious findings. Cadence family with ASSUMPTION-117.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-242

**15a result:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak-Moderate)
**15b result:** CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Designer-unaware twin of ASSUMPTION-220 (MONITOR-229). Topic lists are faithful proxies for *topic*, not for the resolution/significance that defines a PRS; surrogate-fidelity and summarization-faithfulness literature show systematic divergence, and the Medium cap labels but does not correct systematic proxy bias. MONITOR rather than REVISE because the challenge is Moderate (not Strong) and the fix is cheap and scoped (leave resolution/significance unfilled pending transcript). Extra care applied per 15c (PRESUMPTION: designer was unaware).

**What would change disposition:** RESOLVE once topic-list PRS are scoped to topic-level fields with resolution/significance gated on transcript verification; REVISE if labeled-sample discordance is high or such fields are incorporated unverified. Tracks with MONITOR-229.

**PROVENANCE:**

### STALE-MONITOR-FLAGS (3 — first formal flags issued by 15d)

Three items reached cycle 4 this run. Per agent definition (4+ cycles with no change in evidence), formal STALE-MONITOR-FLAGs are issued. All three share a root cause: the blocker is an un-run empirical/paired test, NOT an unsettled literature — so additional 15a/15b literature cycles are predicted low-yield. Recommendation for all three: DOWNGRADE to LOW-PRIORITY-MONITOR (monthly cadence) AND ESCALATE to Tom (the resolving action is a test design/execution Tom controls, not a search). These confirm the 2026-05-18 stale-watch pre-flag recommendations.

```
  Evidence trajectory: stable (loading-half validated N=1 at 2026-04-18; execution-half still untested across 4 cycles)
  Recommendation: DOWNGRADE to LOW-PRIORITY-MONITOR; ESCALATE to Tom
  Reasoning: Resolution requires a paired loading/execution test that has not been run; this is a test-design gap, not a literature gap, so weekly re-triggering wastes cycles. Monthly cadence + Tom-owned test design is the efficient path.

STALE-MONITOR-FLAG:
  Item: ASSUMPTION-037 (MONITOR-042)
  Cycles completed: 4
  Last disposition: MONITOR since 2026-04-17
  Evidence trajectory: stable (planning-fallacy / hidden-I/O prior untested; pivot-on-arrival confound blocks a clean single-shot observation)
  Recommendation: DOWNGRADE to LOW-PRIORITY-MONITOR; ESCALATE to Tom
  Reasoning: A clean single-shot execution observation has never materialized because the test is confounded by pivot-on-arrival; the fix is a structural test redesign Tom controls, not more literature search.

STALE-MONITOR-FLAG:
  Item: PRESUMPTION-037 (MONITOR-044)
  Cycles completed: 4
  Last disposition: MONITOR since 2026-04-17
  Evidence trajectory: stable (loading-half validated N=1; ordinal-dominance vs direct scheduling still unexamined)
  Recommendation: DOWNGRADE to LOW-PRIORITY-MONITOR; ESCALATE to Tom
  Reasoning: The open question is an ordinal-dominance comparison that no run has produced; recommend disaggregating the claim (loading-half is supported; execution/ordinal-half is the unresolved sub-claim) and handing the test to Tom. Monthly cadence going forward.
```

**Cadence effect of downgrade:** MONITOR-040, MONITOR-042, MONITOR-044 shift Weekly -> Monthly (LOW-PRIORITY-MONITOR). Next 15d check for all three: 2026-06-21. They remain in MONITOR status (only 15c re-disposition via INCORPORATE/REVISE removes them).

This 15d run does not re-evaluate evidence (that is 15c's role after 15a/15b complete the re-triggered searches). Per-item evidence-trajectory blocks are carried forward unchanged. Aggregate trajectory observation: across the 75 processed items, supporting/challenging evidence is reported stable since the 2026-05-17/2026-05-18 cycles (no new sources surfaced in the interim daily pipeline null runs of 2026-05-22). The three cycle-4 items show the classic stale signature — stable evidence, recurring same-source set, no new literature — which is precisely why they are flagged rather than re-queued indefinitely.

### NEW MONITOR ENTRIES (4 — cycle-0 items from 2026-05-24 14a/14b extraction)

### MONITOR-233:
**Date added:** 2026-05-25
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-223
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-01 (joins next scheduled 15d run)

**Statement:** When a MONITOR item reaches cycle 4 with stable evidence and the blocker is an un-run empirical/paired test (not unsettled literature), further weekly literature cycles are low-yield; STALE-flag, downgrade Weekly->Monthly, and escalate to a human for the empirical test.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** The rule rests on two solid legs — diminishing returns / saturation in research synthesis (Cooper & Hedges 2009) and matching evidence-type to question-type (an un-run empirical blocker needs an experiment, not more review) — and the escalate-don't-loop half mirrors mature SRE escalation practice (Beyer et al. 2016). That would lean INCORPORATE. But its escalation leg presumes a reachable human endpoint, which the current review-gate outage contradicts (twin PRESUMPTION-245 -> REVISE-053). INCORPORATE-pending-precondition -> MONITOR.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE once a guaranteed/SLA-backed, exercised human endpoint exists (REVISE-050/053 resolved) and a saturation criterion is validated against >=1 human scan; REVISE if STALE escalations continue to dead-end (literature-stall -> human-stall confirmed operationally).

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-245 (MONITOR-235? no -> REVISE-053), REVISE-050; bears on the 3 first STALE flags (MONITOR-040/042/044); OPEN-066.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-234:
**Date added:** 2026-05-25
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-224
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-01 (joins next scheduled 15d run)

**Statement:** The connectivity/orphan metric should exclude `architecture/lit_search_results/` (machine-generated, unrouted) so the orphan count tracks real routing progress.

**15a result:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Legitimate measurement hygiene (excluding machine-generated, by-construction non-linkable files is defensible scope definition; DeVellis 2016) is in genuine tension with a Goodhart/surrogation risk (Goodhart 1975; Campbell 1979; Manheim & Garrabrant 2018): editing the metric's scope in the direction that improves the metric is the textbook 'measure becomes target' move. Contested -> MONITOR.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE if the exclusion rule is pre-registered on construct grounds (independent of its effect on the current count) and BOTH included/excluded counts are reported; REVISE if the metric is re-scoped to hit a target value.

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-246 (MONITOR-236); Goodhart family with PRESUMPTION-201; renewed Tom-decision candidate.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-235:
**Date added:** 2026-05-25
**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-244
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-01 (joins next scheduled 15d run)

**Statement:** Treating same-week thematic convergence as real tradition-level unity (ASSUMPTION-222) presumes the convergence is homology in the sources, not an artifact of one shared intake pipeline/batch/method.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** The presumption's worry — same-week/same-pipeline convergence may be a common-method-variance artifact, not tradition-level homology — is exactly the mono-method-bias concern foundational in measurement (Campbell & Fiske 1959; Podsakoff et al. 2003). Well-supported as a caution. The counter (convergence CAN be genuine when independence holds) keeps it from being a refutation of ASSUMPTION-222; it is the documented caveat to that claim (REVISE-052).

**What would change disposition:** Resolve via a cheap discriminant test: independently re-elicit each source (different agent/occasion/prompt). If the bridge co-emerges, method-variance is ruled out (folds into ASSUMPTION-222 INCORPORATE); if not, ASSUMPTION-222 REVISE is confirmed.

**Coupling:** Designer-unaware twin of ASSUMPTION-222 (REVISE-052).

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-236:
**Date added:** 2026-05-25
**Source item:** PRESUMPTION-246
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION
**Priority:** Low-Medium
**Cadence:** Monthly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-25 (joins next scheduled 15d run)

**Statement:** Excluding lit_search_results/ so the orphan count "tracks routing" (ASSUMPTION-224) presumes backlink-density is a valid proxy for integration at all -- but much of the corpus is operational/archival and never meant to be linked, so the metric may measure machine-log growth, not integration failure.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Weak-Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** The worry that backlink density may not validly proxy integration — because much of the corpus is operational/archival and never meant to be linked — is well-grounded in Goodhart's Law and construct-validity theory (Cronbach & Meehl 1955). The counter is that connectivity is a useful-if-coarse signal (PageRank/centrality), so the fix is contextualization, not abandonment. Net: a valid caution, low stakes, monthly cadence.

**What would change disposition:** Validate the proxy against a hand-coded integration sample; a reasonable correlation licenses continued (caveated) use. Pair with ASSUMPTION-224's pre-registered exclusion + dual-count reporting.

**Coupling:** Designer-unaware twin of ASSUMPTION-224 (MONITOR-234); Goodhart family with PRESUMPTION-201.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

This batch dispositions 24 cycle-0 items: 11 from the 2026-05-25 14a/14b batch (ASSUMPTION-225..229 + PRESUMPTION-248..253) and 13 from the 2026-05-26 14a/14b batch (ASSUMPTION-230..236 + PRESUMPTION-254..259). Result: 0 INCORPORATE / 19 MONITOR / 5 REVISE. See lit_search_returns.md 2026-05-27 entry for full DISPOSITION records.

### MONITOR-237:
**Date added:** 2026-05-27
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-225
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-03

**Statement:** A 34-file / ~90-PRS-triplet / 12-tradition ingestion is too large and error-prone to execute unattended at the tail of the daily cycle; it belongs in a focused, ideally attended session.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Moderate-Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Weak-Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** SRE / continuous-delivery literature supports attended supervision for large heterogeneous batches (Beyer 2016; Humble & Farley 2010); cognitive-ergonomics literature supports the chunking limit (Reason 1990). But the 15b STEELMAN is sharp: what makes the bulk-op safe is idempotency + rollback + observability ENGINEERING, not attention per se. Until those preconditions are engineered, "attended" is a substitute for engineering work undone. INCORPORATE-pending-precondition.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE when (a) idempotency + rollback + observability engineered for the ingest pipeline, OR (b) an attended-session cadence/SLA is in place. REVISE if defer-to-attended turns into permanent deferral (couples PRESUMPTION-248 → REVISE-056).

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-248 (REVISE-056); OPEN-066; cluster with the broader FLAG I deferred-to-attended pattern.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

---
### MONITOR-238:
**Date added:** 2026-05-27
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-226
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-03

**Statement:** A daily-walk Chat conversation on a no-Cowork-session day should count as an interactive Tom session for daily-shape framing.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Moderate)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Symmetric mixed evidence. HCI / conversation-analysis literature supports counting purposeful exchange as interaction regardless of surface (Olson & Olson 2000; ISO 9241). But reproducibility / open-science literature (Munafò et al. 2017) requires machine-captured artifacts for some claim-types; paraphrased capture has documented divergence (Pitkin 1999; Maynez 2020). The right resolution is sub-typing the interaction class (Cowork / Chat-walk / Chat-text) with explicit fidelity tags, not a binary count.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE if interaction-class is sub-typed with fidelity tag AND extraction-eligibility distinguished from cadence-counting. REVISE if undifferentiated counting causes downstream extraction errors.

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-249 (MONITOR-247); fidelity-vs-source family (PRESUMPTION-241, PRESUMPTION-247).

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-239:
**Date added:** 2026-05-27
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-227
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Low-Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-03

**Statement:** A daily-sync delivery should LEAD with whichever finding closes the loop on the morning walk's open question.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Weak)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Communication-theory support is strong (Minto; Grice's Maxim of Relation; Clark & Brennan's grounding work). Challenge is weak (predictability vs personalization trade-off). Would lean INCORPORATE on evidence balance, BUT the rule's operative precondition — reliable loop-detection — is itself contested (PRESUMPTION-250 → MONITOR-248). The lead becomes loop-CLAIMING rather than loop-CLOSING when detection is unreliable. INCORPORATE-pending-precondition.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE when loop-detection accuracy is measured and exceeds a documented threshold (e.g., 80% reviewer-confirmed); REVISE if detection-fail rate is high.

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-250 (MONITOR-248); communicative-design family.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-240:
**Date added:** 2026-05-27
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-228
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-03

**Statement:** The C2A2 community-model GPRS articulation is structurally homologous to Levin & Lyons' "shared model of relative scarcities"; the price-system-as-template thesis supplies a peer-reviewed coordination mechanism.

**15a result:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Hayek/Ostrom/Friston/Levin&Lyons constitute a coherent multi-disciplinary basis for the price-system-as-information thesis being substrate-portable in principle; but the analogy carries known failure modes (Stiglitz, Akerlof, Granovetter embeddedness critique). Domain-transfer caveats (Gentner 1983) require explicit source-target mapping. Mixed evidence; target-state-conditional.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE once an explicit source-target mapping is documented AND demonstrated to apply at C2A2's target-state architecture. REVISE if the embeddedness/trust mechanisms are demonstrably substituted-away by importing the analogy.

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-251 (MONITOR-249); architectural-transfer family; bears on Loughran-master integration project.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-241:
**Date added:** 2026-05-27
**Source item:** ASSUMPTION-230
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION
**Priority:** Medium
**Cadence:** Weekly
**Next 15d check:** 2026-06-03

**Statement:** When Gmail decision-email body and review-page state disagree, review-page state is authoritative; the email body is non-authoritative until the decision-email generator is fixed.

**15a result:** SUPPORTED (Strong)
**15b result:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Weak-Moderate)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** System-of-record selection is the dominant data-governance pattern (Kimball & Ross 2013; Fowler PEAA). But PRESUMPTION-254 (UI-misfire 3-Wright case) shows UI can also fail; 2-way priority is brittle. The cleanest resolution is 3-way reconciliation (intent + UI + email + log), not UI-as-authoritative. Would lean INCORPORATE on the FOR direction but PRESUMPTION-254 keeps it in MONITOR.

**What would change disposition:** INCORPORATE once 3-way reconciliation is implemented OR the underlying generator-divergence is fixed. REVISE if UI-misfires recur with the 2-way rule in place.

**Coupling:** Couples PRESUMPTION-254 (MONITOR-251); approval-workflow family; decision-candidate DECISION-048.

**PROVENANCE:**
  Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

**Item type:** ASSUMPTION

**15a:** SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)

**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Helpful and cheap; the gap is the missing verification/failure-mode. MONITOR with a freshness-check remedy; couples PRESUMPTION-282 (REVISE-075).

### MONITOR-285 — ASSUMPTION-262
**Statement:** 16/16 logic validation establishes 1.6 parser-level correctness; visual/fade behavior is a separate foreground-tab verification deferred behind the hold.
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION (stated)
**15a:** SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Logic/visual separation is sound, but '16/16 establishes correctness' overstates coverage adequacy, and the fade bug is a counterexample to logic-pass=working.

### MONITOR-287 — PRESUMPTION-281
**Statement:** [inferred] 'One COLORS line + regen' presumes registration stays cheap at N=33/100 -- palette distinctness, regen time, ~20MB HTML size not examined as scaling costs.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Edit cost is O(1) but distinct-color budget (~10-12) and growing ~26MB artifact size are real scaling walls below the project's N targets. Not yet binding, so not REVISE, but a known design ceiling.
**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Scaling limit is real and well-evidenced but not yet hit; cheap to plan for. MONITOR-HIGH with explicit color-ceiling and size flags.

**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-288 — ASSUMPTION-263
**Statement:** Re-authenticating claude.ai in the extension's Chrome profile is the single fix that restores BOTH daily-sync directions (morning intake scrape + evening cowork→chat delivery); one re-login unblocks both.
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION (stated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Re-login is the canonical first action for shared-credential expiry, but "single fix that holds" is challenged by 3rd-cycle recurrence and extension-session fragility. Cheaply testable on next attended login.
**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Low-cost-to-verify operational hypothesis; not yet verified (so not INCORPORATE) and needs no design change yet (so not REVISE) — just an empirical check.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-07. Priority HIGH (gates the whole loop). What would change: BOTH directions recover and hold ≥2 cycles -> INCORPORATE; recurrence after re-login -> REVISE (multi-cause RCA). Couples PRESUMPTION-288.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-289 — PRESUMPTION-288
**Statement:** [inferred] The daily-sync architecture uses a single shared transport (Claude-in-Chrome on a live claude.ai session) for BOTH loop directions with no fallback — a common-mode SPOF.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate)
**Net:** A single shared transport is a defensible KISS/YAGNI choice for a low-stakes personal pipeline, but it is a textbook common-mode SPOF that has actually fired 3 cycles. "Add redundancy" is a genuine cost-benefit call, not a clear refutation.
**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Contested (stakes vs realized failure); err toward MONITOR over REVISE since redundancy-worth is a human cost-benefit judgment. Priority HIGH because the SPOF is active and the remedy is shared with REVISE-080.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-07. Priority HIGH. What would change: a 4th outage cycle, or evidence the outage silences the alert path too -> REVISE toward a diverse degraded-mode channel. Couples ASSUMPTION-263, PRESUMPTION-287/289; 2026-05-31 systemic-risk cluster.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### CONSOLIDATED ESCALATION TO TOM — HIGH/CRITICAL stale cluster

*19 of the 36 downgraded items are HIGH-priority (MONITOR-001 also CRITICAL-risk: role-based bias amplification). ### MONITOR-290 — PRESUMPTION-292
**Statement:** [inferred] The honesty layer presumes the agent will reliably notice and override a degraded session's false success read; there is no structural guard independent of the agent choosing to re-verify — fail-loud is a disposition, not an enforced interlock.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** SUPPORTED (Moderate-Strong) | **15b:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)
**Net:** The hierarchy of controls ranks engineered/forcing-function guards above administrative ones (behavioral norms) precisely because the latter depend on operator compliance — strongly supporting the concern. But the hierarchy is a prioritization conditioned on feasibility and stakes; for a recoverable, single-operator pipeline a behavioral norm may be proportionate (YAGNI). Genuinely contested.
**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Whether to add a mechanical interlock vs. rely on the fail-loud norm is a cost-benefit human judgment, not a clear refutation — err toward MONITOR over REVISE (15c heuristic). Priority MEDIUM-HIGH because the guard protects the honesty layer itself and the degraded-session condition is exactly when a behavior-only guard is least reliable.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-07. Priority MEDIUM-HIGH. What would change: any past or future degraded-session false "success" that the behavioral norm failed to catch -> REVISE toward a lightweight forcing function (block asserting "verified" without an out-of-band confirmation token). Couples ASSUMPTION-264 (PREMISE-045), PRESUMPTION-293 (REVISE-084, out-of-band requirement); 2026-06-02 no-independent-vantage cluster.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

**Net:** Curation efficacy is supported (medallion/Great Expectations; Wikipedia notability). The NORMATIVE claim is challenged by participation-inequality (Nielsen 90-9-1) and visibility-as-power scholarship (Bowker & Star; D'Ignazio & Klein): articulation tracks resources/literacy, so an articulation gate reproduces inequality and hides emerging/under-resourced communities while presenting as neutral.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** A normative PRESUMPTION cannot be INCORPORATED on literature alone — it is a value decision for Tom — and it is not yet a demonstrated harm to REVISE; but it is too consequential (who is visible) to leave implicit. -> MONITOR with HIGH priority and an explicit "surface to Tom" action. Cross-listed in the revision_flags SYSTEMIC-RISK note (validity-smuggling pair).
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-13. Priority HIGH. What would change: surface the value choice explicitly to Tom (decide the visibility policy on purpose); measure whether admission correlates with community resource proxies and whether excluded seeds skew under-resourced -> if yes, REVISE toward equity-aware admission (assisted articulation / provisional seed visibility); if the policy is deliberately endorsed, INCORPORATE the stated rationale. Couples ASSUMPTION-276 (MONITOR-303).
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-306 — PRESUMPTION-310
**Statement:** [inferred] Accepting "zero Civic↔Scientific cross-links" as honest signal presumes TF-IDF lexical similarity is a valid proxy for genuine inter-community relatedness; the verification confirmed the count, not the construct validity of TF-IDF edges.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** TF-IDF/cosine is a validated relatedness baseline within a shared vocabulary domain (Salton & Buckley; standard IR). Cross-domain it is semantically blind (Reimers & Gurevych; TF-IDF semantic-blindness literature): Civic and Scientific registers differ, so near-zero cross-links is indistinguishable from related-but-lexically-divergent. The verification established reliability (the count), not construct validity (that edges measure relatedness).
**Reasoning for MONITOR:** Conditional on a checkable fact (re-run edges with embeddings; do semantic cross-links appear that TF-IDF missed?) -> MONITOR. Not INCORPORATE ("zero = honest signal" is construct-unverified cross-domain); not REVISE (TF-IDF is a defensible first pass, no defect shown). PRESUMPTION + epistemic -> extra care.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-13. Priority MEDIUM. What would change: build embedding-based edges (e.g., Sentence-BERT) and compare cross-domain counts. Embeddings ≈ TF-IDF (still ~0) -> INCORPORATE the "honest signal" reading; embeddings surface cross-domain links -> REVISE to treat near-zero TF-IDF as "lexically disjoint, relatedness unknown" and report the construct caveat. Couples the PRS-edge-validity family.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-307 — PRESUMPTION-311
**Statement:** [inferred] Deferring the join to the P3 promotion pipeline presumes curated communities and directory records are the same kind of object that should eventually share an id space; the alternative — categorically distinct, should never join — was never raised.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Lifecycle/MDM models (CRM lead→customer; medallion promotion; golden records) license a shared id space only when it is the SAME entity across stages. Presuming that for curated-community vs directory-seed conflates association with identity (Chen ER model; Bowker & Star; MDM over-merge caution), and the distinct-kinds alternative was never raised. Conceptual twin of PRESUMPTION-306 (feasibility): 306 = CAN they join, 311 = SHOULD they.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** A PRESUMPTION that pre-commits an ontology (one object vs two) is a modeling decision for Tom, contingent on the 306 linkage result -> MONITOR with HIGH priority and an explicit "raise the suppressed alternative" action. Not REVISE independently (it rides on 306, already REVISE-089); not INCORPORATE (identity unproven).
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-13. Priority HIGH. What would change: decide the entity model explicitly — one object across maturity (shared id space) vs two associated kinds (link table, never merged). Prefer association-by-link until identity is positively established (MDM caution). Gated by the 306 result. Couples PRESUMPTION-306 (REVISE-089), ASSUMPTION-276 (MONITOR-303), OPEN-075.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-308 — ASSUMPTION-278
**Statement:** Merging the 156 curated communities into the Cards directory under their own CC-xxx ids (graph becomes a literal id-subset of the cards) is the correct way to make the directory⊇graph relationship true and to unblock the deferred cross-navigation hand-off.
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION (stated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Sound integration mechanics (surrogate keys / MDM golden ids) make the subset relation ADDRESSABLE, but not TRUE; validity rides on the identity question, which the 2026-06-05 measurement (0 id / 3 name / 5 host) leaves near the signal floor.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** The technique is legitimate (not REVISE on its own) but is the enacted vehicle of a contested identity (PRESUMPTION-312 → REVISE-091); cannot INCORPORATE until the linkage is measured.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-14. Priority HIGH. What would change: the REVISE-089/REVISE-091 record-linkage result — overlap confirmed → the merge is correct, lean INCORPORATE; overlap stays ~0 → the merge asserted identity by fiat, model as association (link table) and unwind the shared id space. Cross-listed in manufactured-identity SYSTEMIC-RISK. Couples PRESUMPTION-312, prior REVISE-089 / MONITOR-307.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-309 — ASSUMPTION-279
**Statement:** Cards and Graph are "two surfaces, one instrument" and mutually upbuilding — the directory feeds the graph (well-articulated seeds earn their place) and the graph gives the directory its purpose; "breadth invites, depth reveals — each makes the other more truthful."
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION (stated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate complementarity / Weak for the stronger claim) | **15b:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)
**Net:** The functional-complementarity core is already PREMISE-051 (via ASSUMPTION-275). The residual content — purely-reinforcing, "earned place," "each makes the other more truthful" — is a tiered-visibility value claim CMV theory does not license and PRESUMPTION-316 challenges.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (MEDIUM):** Consistency requires NOT re-INCORPORATING a duplicate of PREMISE-051; the residual "earned/more truthful" claim is contested and normative → MONITOR rather than INCORPORATE/REVISE.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-14. Priority MEDIUM. What would change: the 316 stigma test (do carded-only communities suffer an attention/representation penalty?) — no penalty → the benign "upbuilding" reading holds; penalty found → REVISE the framing. Couples PRESUMPTION-314/316, PREMISE-051.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-310 — PRESUMPTION-314
**Statement:** [inferred] Calling the curated 156 "communities articulated to a quality bar" presumes a quality gate exists and was applied to them, when membership is curatorial fiat and "no community has approved its record" — instantiating the opposite of the earned-membership telos the same doc advances.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak-Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Curatorial selection is a legitimate quality signal in the EDITORIAL sense, but the phrase credits the COMMUNITIES with a property produced by the CURATOR (no independent criterion, no approval) — a construct-validity error (metric produced by the measurer) and aspirational-vs-operative inversion.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** A value/measurement claim smuggled into a "quality" descriptor is a decision for Tom (relabel honestly vs define an independent community-side criterion), not a literature-settleable defect.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-14. Priority HIGH. What would change: relabel "curator-selected (not community-approved)" OR define and apply an independent community-side articulation criterion. Validity-smuggling family; couples PRESUMPTION-308, 316. Surface to Tom.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-311 — PRESUMPTION-315
**Statement:** [inferred] Dispositioning the app.js:1314 error as a stale buffer artifact presumes single-reload non-reproduction + one positive handler-fires check is a complete exoneration; reachability of the throwing init-state from real user sequences was not established.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak-Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Vanish-on-reload is the heisenbug signature of a timing/init-order-sensitive defect; a single non-repro + one positive path does not establish unreachability of the throwing init-state. "Stale buffer" is a plausible hypothesis, not a verified diagnosis.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (MEDIUM):** Resolvable by a cheap, decisive check; unresolved at autonomous run time → MONITOR, not closed/exonerated and not REVISE (no realized harm).
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-14. Priority MEDIUM. What would change: repeated/randomized + cold-cache reload attempts and added init telemetry — no recurrence under varied orderings → close as stale-buffer; recurrence → diagnose the init-order race. Keep OPEN-pending-recurrence with a guard rather than closed.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-312 — PRESUMPTION-316
**Statement:** [inferred] The "mutually upbuilding … each makes the other more truthful" framing presumes Cards and Graph are purely reinforcing and ignores the failure mode where graph-absence stigmatizes the carded-only majority (graph-presence as visible earned status).
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak-Moderate) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Status-tier and participation-inequality literature show visible earned status produces lower-tier stigma and discourages the unseen majority; graph-absence reads as deficiency (visibility-as-power), contradicting "purely reinforcing."
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** A visibility value choice (graphed = legitimate) too consequential to leave implicit but not yet a demonstrated harm to REVISE.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-14. Priority HIGH. What would change: measure carded-only vs graphed community treatment/representation; design graph membership as a data-coverage state, not a merit badge. Couples PRESUMPTION-314 (MONITOR-310) and ASSUMPTION-279 (MONITOR-309). Surface to Tom.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

## MONITOR — run 2026-06-08 (2026-06-07 PRS-connectome EOD batch)

### MONITOR-313 — ASSUMPTION-284
**Statement:** The right safety split is "approved data auto-publishes, generator/template code changes are gated for human visual review."
**Item type:** ASSUMPTION (stated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Moderate; asymmetry only) | **15b:** PARTIALLY-CHALLENGED (Moderate)
**Net:** "Code needs more review" is supported by blast-radius reasoning, but "data auto-publishes unreviewed" inverts a documented risk ordering — data regressions are the quietest, latest-detected, most-propagating failures and publish as fact. Floor (asymmetry) and ceiling (data review-exempt) come apart.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (MEDIUM):** Do not INCORPORATE a clean split whose data-half depends on PRESUMPTION-319 (REVISE-094, challenged) and whose enacting design failed in a non-capable environment (317). The asymmetry is right but "no review" should be "auto-CHECKED."
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-15. Priority MEDIUM. What would change: replace the unreviewed-data path with AUTOMATED data-quality gates (schema + invariants + diff-magnitude/anomaly thresholds) → lean INCORPORATE the gated asymmetry; leave data genuinely unchecked → lean REVISE. Pairs PRESUMPTION-319 (REVISE-094); secondary "derived-data-review-exempt" SYSTEMIC cluster. Surface to Tom.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14a; Chain: [14a -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-314 — PRESUMPTION-318
**Statement:** [inferred] Building the auto-push task before probing whether the sandbox could push presumed capabilities instead of checking them (violates Rules 1/8).
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** NO-SUPPORT-FOUND (None) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Building a capability-dependent automation before probing the capability is a recognized, costly anti-pattern (walking-skeleton, fail-fast, shift-left, pre-mortem) and a direct violation of the project's own Rules 1 and 8. It is the procedural CAUSE of 317, not an independent design defect.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (HIGH):** Clear, self-evidently supported lesson, but a process discipline to ADOPT (capability-probe-first / dry-run as a standing pre-step), not a literature premise to INCORPORATE nor a defect distinct from 317's REVISE.
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-15. Priority HIGH. What would change: adopt probe-before-build as a non-skippable pre-step for any environment-capability-dependent automation → close. Core of the environment-capability-mismatch SYSTEMIC-RISK; coupled to PRESUMPTION-317 (REVISE-093) and 320 (MONITOR-315).
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-315 — PRESUMPTION-320
**Statement:** [inferred] Handing the user blind multi-command shell blocks presumes the agent's model of the user's repo state is accurate enough to script state-mutating sequences.
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** PARTIALLY-SUPPORTED (Weak-Moderate; idempotent form only) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate-Strong)
**Net:** Prepared operator sequences are endorsed only in idempotent, state-checking, or declarative-convergent form. A blind imperative block bets the repo on the agent's unverified state model (dirty tree, diverged branch, partial prior run); a mid-sequence failure strands the repo in an unknown partial state with no safe rerun, and a non-idempotent retry destroys work.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (MEDIUM-HIGH):** Resolvable by a concrete low-cost practice change rather than a literature-settleable defect or an enacted-and-failed design (no realized harm yet).
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Weekly; next 15d 2026-06-15. Priority MEDIUM-HIGH. What would change: adopt "no blind state-mutating blocks" — make each step idempotent/safe-to-fail, inspect-then-act (print git status/branch, stop on surprise), one reversible step over a compound block, never blind force-push/reset → close. Couples PRESUMPTION-317 (the agent also mismodels the environment). Environment-capability-mismatch SYSTEMIC-RISK.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING

### MONITOR-316 — PRESUMPTION-321
**Statement:** [inferred] The system presumes "automation day" and "attended session" are mutually exclusive day-types (today's attended PRS session was mislabeled "automation-only").
**Item type:** PRESUMPTION (unstated)
**15a:** NO-SUPPORT-FOUND (Weak; MECE ideal only) | **15b:** CHALLENGED (Moderate; low stakes)
**Net:** "Attended" and "automation-ran" are orthogonal dimensions, not a partition; the binary fails mutual-exclusivity (today's attended session on a nominal automation day is the overlap case), and forcing it mislabels overlap days, biasing downstream activity metrics (label-noise). Real but minor measurement corruption.
**Reasoning for MONITOR (LOW):** Genuine taxonomy defect with a clear cheap fix but low stakes; not REVISE (no design dependency at risk), not closed (mislabel recurs until the taxonomy changes).
**Detail / cadence / priority:** Cadence Monthly; next 15d 2026-07-08. Priority LOW. What would change: model day-type as NON-EXCLUSIVE tags (a day may be both "attended" and "automation-ran"), or add a "mixed" category, derived from actual activity; backfill the mislabeled 2026-06-07 day → close.
**PROVENANCE:** Origin: 14b; Chain: [14b -> 15a, 15b -> 15c]; Current status: MONITORING
